# Third-party notices

Dewline.app contains the software below. `scripts/notices.sh` writes this file from `core/Cargo.lock`,
`Package.resolved`, the bundled fonts and the code syntaxes; edit the script, not this file.

## MPL-2.0: UniFFI

The UniFFI runtime (uniffi 0.32.2, uniffi_core 0.32.2, uniffi_pipeline 0.32.2) is under the Mozilla Public License 2.0 and ships in the app
unmodified. Its source is at https://github.com/mozilla/uniffi-rs and on crates.io under the versions named here.

## Apache-2.0: OpenAI Codex

`core/crates/cli/src/patch.rs` ports the patch parser and line matching of Codex's apply-patch
(codex-rs/apply-patch at 970111f6 in https://github.com/openai/codex) under the Apache License 2.0, whose text is
below. Its NOTICE reads: OpenAI Codex, Copyright 2025 OpenAI.

### ring 0.17.14 (LICENSE)

```text
*ring* uses an "ISC" license, like BoringSSL used to use, for new code
files. See LICENSE-other-bits for the text of that license.

Apache 2.0 license. Some code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the ISC
license. In each case, the license info is at the top of the file.

for the license to code that was sourced from the once_cell project.
```
